Title: Das Leben Muhammed's nach Muhammed Ibn Ishâk bearbeitet von Abd el-Malik Ibn Hischâm, Erster Band, Text, Erster Theil
Creator: Ibn Ishaq, as edited by Ibn Hisham; edited by Ferdinand Wüstenfeld (title page)
Publisher: Göttingen: Dieterichsche Universitäts-Buchhandlung, 1858 (title page, read on the page image; the OCR reads 1358)
Publication Date: 1858 (title page)
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1858); the archive.org record is tagged NOT_IN_COPYRIGHT
Language: ara
Source: archive.org item p1daslebenmuhamm01ibnhuoft, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-13 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (1,011,222) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1858, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: fair to poor for the Arabic. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: The Arabic text of the Sira, first part. Khadija and Waraqa at about OCR line 20563 and 23925; the Najran story of Faymiyun at about 31234. The scan runs from the back of the book.
